# File-by-file audit ledger

Baseline `1794dd27fad87e8f0cc6f4923d37eedf713783ed`; **1,471 tracked files**, exactly one row per path. Every baseline size and SHA-256 rechecked after review. Source unchanged. See [audit report](AUDIT_REPORT.md), [coverage proof](coverage-proof.json), [CSV](FILE_AUDIT.csv), and [Apple requirements](APPLE_WATCH_REQUIREMENTS.md).

Methods are literal: authored-source/prose review, structured all-record validation, full media decoding or format checks. None of the binary rows implies a new visual, exercise-technique or hardware review. Finding IDs are scoped by review part (for example tooling:T01 versus tests:T01). The JSON and CSV preserve all hashes/byte counts.

| Path | Review part and method | Checked scope | Result / findings | Limits |
